bootable/libbootloader/gbl/libgbl/src/partition.rs-165-    // blocks, some of which may want to modify the GPT. Tasks that want to modify
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/libgbl/src/partition.rs-166-    // the GPT need to wait for `partitions` to be available for mutable borrow.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/libgbl/src/partition.rs-167-    // This causes `partitions` to act as a de facto lock.

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-196-the current device state.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-197-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-198-### Description
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-199-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-200-Caller calls this interface to allow the firmware to inspect, update, or move
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md:201:the buffers returned by `GetPartitionBuffer()`. Caller can call this API after
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-202-loading new images into the buffers to notify the firmware to process them.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-203-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-204-Caller can also call this API with `SyncPreloaded` set to true to request the
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-205-firmware to re-sync preloaded partition data. Firmware should either re-load the
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-206-partitions if supported, or invalidate existing ones by clearing the `PRELOADED`
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md:207:bit or returning `EFI_NOT_FOUND` for future calls to `GetPartitionBuffer()`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-208-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-209-After calling this method, all buffers previously obtained from
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md:210:`GetPartitionBuffer()` must be considered invalid.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-211-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-212-### Status Codes Returned
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-213-

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-215-| :---------------------- | :--------------------------- |
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-216-| `EFI_SUCCESS`           | Sync completed successfully. |
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-217-| `EFI_DEVICE_ERROR`      | An internal error occurred.  |
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_memory_protocol.md-218-| `EFI_INVALID_PARAMETER` | `Self` is invalid.           |

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-64-GBL uses this buffer as download buffer in fastboot mode.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-65-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-66-GBL queries the buffer by calling
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-67-[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.GetBootBuffer()][get_boot_buffer] with parameter
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-68-`BootBufferType` set to `FASTBOOT_DOWNLOAD`. If the buffer is not provided, GBL
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-69-allocates a default 512MB memory via EFI allocation.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-70-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-71-## Partition Read Buffers
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-72-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-73-By default, GBL allocates memory for reading disk partitions when assembling
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-74-boot images. Firmware can override this for individual partitions by providing a
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-75-dedicated read buffer via the
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:76:[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.GetPartitionBuffer()][get_partition_buffer] API.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-77-Before reading a partition, GBL queries the buffer using the slotless partition
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-78-name. For example, before reading `"boot_a/b"` partition, GBL calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:79:[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.GetPartitionBuffer()][get_partition_buffer] with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-80-parameter `BaseName` set to `"boot"`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-81-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-82-Firmware can also provide preloaded partition data in the buffer. The
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-83-`PRELOADED` bit in the output parameter `Flag` should be set in this case. GBL
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-84-skips reading the partition when preloaded data is available. This can be used
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-85-for optimizing boot performance or overriding target boot images (images still
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-86-need to pass AVB verification in locked mode).
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-87-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-88-If GBL has entered fastboot mode in the same session, preloaded partition data
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-89-may be outdated since new images may have been flashed and active slot may have
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-90-been changed. In this case, GBL calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:91:[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer()][sync_partition_buffer] with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-92-parameter `SyncPreloaded` set to true after exiting fastboot mode and before
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-93-querying any partition buffer. Firmware should either reload the partitions, or
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-94-simply clear the `PRELOADED` bit to instruct GBL to read from disk by itself.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-95-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-96-After GBL finishes assembling kernel/ramdisk/FDT from partition images, and
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-97-before fixing up FDT/bootconfig via
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-98-[GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL][gbl_os_configuration_protocol], GBL calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:99:[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer()][sync_partition_buffer] with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-100-parameter `SyncPreloaded` set to false. Firmware can take this chance to inspect
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-101-newly loaded images in the provided partition buffers and determines the needed
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-102-FDT/bootconfig fixup if needed.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-103-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-104-The following summarizes the order of events discussed above.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-105-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-106-1. If GBL previously entered fastboot, calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:107:   [GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer(SyncPreloaded=TRUE)][sync_partition_buffer]
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-108-2. Queries partition buffer with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:109:   [GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.GetPartitionBuffer()][get_partition_buffer].
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-110-3. Reads, verifies partition images and assembles kernel/ramdisk/FDT.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-111-4. Calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:112:   [GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer(SyncPreloaded=FALSE)][sync_partition_buffer].
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-113-5. Fixes up FDT and bootconfig via DT_FIXUP and
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-114-   [GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL][gbl_os_configuration_protocol].
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-115-6. Boot.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-116-

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-118-

===== ANDROID BOOT FLOW DOCUMENTATION =====
# GBL Android Boot Flow

GBL boots Android in the following stages.

1. **Partition Discovery**: GBL scans block devices and syncs their GPT
   partition tables.
2. **Boot Mode & Slot Selection**: GBL reads the bootloader control
   block (BCB) in the `misc` partition and the platform one-shot boot mode
   (`GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL`) to decide whether to enter Fastboot,
   Recovery, or Normal boot, and gets the current A/B slot from
   `GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL`.
3. **Load & Verify**:
   - Loads `boot`, `init_boot`, `vendor_boot`, `vendor_kernel_boot`, `dtb`,
     `dtbo`, `pvmfw` (when AVF is supported), and any platform-specific
     partitions requested via `GBL_EFI_AVB_PROTOCOL` into firmware-designated
     buffers (`GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL`).
   - Verifies loaded partitions via Android Verified Boot (`libavb` and
     `GBL_EFI_AVB_PROTOCOL`), enforcing lock state and rollback index updates.
   - Selects device tree components via `GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL`,
     applies the selected overlays, and applies final FDT fixups via
     `EFI_DT_FIXUP_PROTOCOL`.
   - Assembles kernel command line and `bootconfig` parameters (including AVB,
     slot, and AVF parameters) and applies platform fixups via
     `GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL`.
   - When AVF is enabled, builds the `pvmfw` configuration (a DICE handover
     derived from the vendor DICE handover and a VM reference device tree
     containing the Secretkeeper public key) via `GBL_EFI_AVF_PROTOCOL`.
4. **Kernel Handoff**: GBL calls
   `GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL.HandleLoadedOs()` when implemented, then exits
   UEFI Boot Services and jumps to the kernel entry point.

===== END OF REPORT =====
